package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.util.Log
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.common.cleanUpMediapipeTaskErrorMessage
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Accelerator
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.ConfigKey
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.DEFAULT_TOPK
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.DEFAULT_TOPP
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.MAX_IMAGE_COUNT
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Model
import com.google.mediapipe.framework.image.BitmapImageBuilder
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.GraphOptions
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.LlmInference
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.LlmInferenceSession
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone
import java.util.UUID

private const val TAG = "DebugLlmHelper"

data class LlmStats(
    val timeToFirstToken: Long, // en ms
    val tokensPerSecond: Float,
    val totalTokens: Int,
    val totalInferenceTime: Long // en ms
)

data class LlmResult(
    val partialText: String,
    val stats: LlmStats?,
    val done: Boolean
)

typealias ResultListener = (result: LlmResult) -> Unit
typealias CleanUpListener = () -> Unit

data class LlmModelInstance(val engine: LlmInference, var session: LlmInferenceSession)

object DebugLlmHelper {
    private val cleanUpListeners: MutableMap<String, CleanUpListener> = mutableMapOf()

    fun initialize(context: Context, model: Model, onDone: (String) -> Unit) {
        val modelPath = if (model.imported) model.url else {
            val modelBaseDir = File(context.getExternalFilesDir(null), "debug_models")
            val modelDir = File(modelBaseDir, model.name)
            val taskFileName = model.downloadFileName.removeSuffix(".zip")
            File(modelDir, taskFileName).absolutePath
        }

        Log.d(TAG, "Initializing '${model.name}' with path: $modelPath")

        val maxTokens = model.getIntConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.MAX_TOKENS, defaultValue = DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS_DEBUG)
        Log.d(TAG, "Setting maxTokens to: $maxTokens for model ${model.name}")

        val accelerator =
            model.getStringConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.ACCELERATOR, defaultValue = Accelerator.CPU.label)
        val preferredBackend =
            when (accelerator) {
                Accelerator.GPU.label -> LlmInference.Backend.GPU
                else -> LlmInference.Backend.CPU
            }
        Log.i(TAG, "Using backend: $preferredBackend")


        val optionsBuilder =
            LlmInference.LlmInferenceOptions.builder()
                .setModelPath(modelPath)
                .setMaxTokens(maxTokens)
                .setPreferredBackend(preferredBackend)
                .setMaxNumImages(if (model.llmSupportImage) MAX_IMAGE_COUNT else 0)
        val options = optionsBuilder.build()

        try {
            val llmInference = LlmInference.createFromOptions(context, options)
            model.instance = LlmModelInstance(engine = llmInference, session = createNewSession(model, llmInference))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            onDone(cleanUpMediapipeTaskErrorMessage(e.message ?: "Unknown error"))
            return
        }
        onDone("")
    }

    private fun createNewSession(model: Model, llmInference: LlmInference, temperatureOverride: Float? = null): LlmInferenceSession {
        val topK = model.getIntConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.TOPK, defaultValue = DEFAULT_TOPK)
        val topP = model.getFloatConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.TOPP, defaultValue = DEFAULT_TOPP)
        val temperature = temperatureOverride ?: model.getFloatConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.TEMPERATURE, defaultValue = DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE)

        Log.d(TAG, "Creating session for '${model.name}' with Temp: $temperature, TopK: $topK, TopP: $topP")

        return LlmInferenceSession.createFromOptions(
            llmInference,
            LlmInferenceSession.LlmInferenceSessionOptions.builder()
                .setTopK(topK)
                .setTopP(topP)
                .setTemperature(temperature)
                .setGraphOptions(
                    GraphOptions.builder()
                        .setEnableVisionModality(model.llmSupportImage)
                        .build()
                )
                .build(),
        )
    }

    fun resetSession(model: Model) {
        if (model.instance == null) return
        val instance = model.instance as LlmModelInstance
        Log.d(TAG, "Resetting session for model '${model.name}'")
        try {
            instance.session.close()
            instance.session = createNewSession(model, instance.engine)
            Log.d(TAG, "Session for '${model.name}' has been reset.")
        } catch(e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to reset session for '${model.name}': ${e.message}")
            cleanUp(model)
        }
    }

    fun cleanUp(model: Model) {
        if (model.instance == null) return

        val instance = model.instance as LlmModelInstance
        try {
            instance.session.close()
            instance.engine.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to close the LLM Inference session: ${e.message}")
        }

        val onCleanUp = cleanUpListeners.remove(model.name)
        onCleanUp?.invoke()
        model.instance = null
        Log.d(TAG, "Clean up for '${model.name}' done.")
    }

    fun runInference(
        model: Model,
        input: String,
        images: List<Bitmap> = listOf(),
        temperature: Float? = null,
        resultListener: ResultListener,
        cleanUpListener: CleanUpListener
    ) {
        val instance = model.instance as? LlmModelInstance ?: return

        if (!cleanUpListeners.containsKey(model.name)) {
            cleanUpListeners[model.name] = cleanUpListener
        }

        val finalTemperature = temperature ?: model.getFloatConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.TEMPERATURE, defaultValue = DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE)

        val sessionToUse = if (temperature != null) {
            Log.d(TAG, "Using temporary session with temperature override: $temperature")
            createNewSession(model, instance.engine, temperature)
        } else {
            instance.session
        }

        var firstTokenTimestamp: Long = -1
        val fullResponse = StringBuilder()
        val inferenceStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

        val inferenceId = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
        val dateFormat = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss'Z'", Locale.getDefault())
        dateFormat.timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        val timestamp = dateFormat.format(Date())

        // BOURDON'S NOTE: Logging prompt to console as requested.
        Log.d(TAG, "--- INFERENCE START (ID: $inferenceId) ---")
        Log.d(TAG, "[PROMPT SENT]:\n$input")

        if (input.trim().isNotEmpty()) {
            sessionToUse.addQueryChunk(input)
        }
        for (image in images) {
            sessionToUse.addImage(BitmapImageBuilder(image).build())
        }

        sessionToUse.generateResponseAsync { partialResult, done ->
            if (firstTokenTimestamp == -1L) {
                firstTokenTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
            }

            fullResponse.append(partialResult)

            if (!done) {
                resultListener(LlmResult(partialResult, null, false))
            } else {
                val totalTimeMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - inferenceStartTime
                val timeToFirst = firstTokenTimestamp - inferenceStartTime
                val totalOutputTokens = instance.engine.sizeInTokens(fullResponse.toString())
                val tokensPerSec = if (totalTimeMs > 0) {
                    (totalOutputTokens.toFloat() / totalTimeMs) * 1000
                } else {
                    0f
                }

                val stats = LlmStats(
                    timeToFirstToken = timeToFirst,
                    tokensPerSecond = tokensPerSec,
                    totalTokens = totalOutputTokens,
                    totalInferenceTime = totalTimeMs
                )
                resultListener(LlmResult(partialResult, stats, true))

                // BOURDON'S NOTE: Logging raw response to console as requested.
                Log.d(TAG, "[RAW RESPONSE (ID: $inferenceId)]:\n${fullResponse.toString()}")
                Log.d(TAG, "--- INFERENCE END (ID: $inferenceId) ---")

                try {
                    val modelConfig = ModelConfiguration(
                        temperature = finalTemperature,
                        topK = model.getIntConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.TOPK, defaultValue = DEFAULT_TOPK),
                        maxTokens = model.getIntConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.MAX_TOKENS, defaultValue = DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS_DEBUG),
                        accelerator = model.getStringConfigValue(key = ConfigKey.ACCELERATOR, defaultValue = Accelerator.CPU.label)
                    )
                    val modelInfo = ModelInfo(name = model.name, configuration = modelConfig)
                    val metadata = InferenceMetadata(latencyMs = stats.totalInferenceTime, tokensPerSecond = stats.tokensPerSecond)

                    val logEntry = InferenceLog(
                        inferenceId = inferenceId,
                        timestamp = timestamp,
                        useCase = "DebugActivityInference",
                        model = modelInfo,
                        prompt = input, // CORRIGÉ
                        rawResponse = fullResponse.toString(), // CORRIGÉ
                        metadata = metadata
                    )
                    InferenceLogger.add(logEntry)
                    Log.i(TAG, "Inference ${logEntry.inferenceId} logged successfully.")
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Failed to create or add inference log.", e)
                }

                if (sessionToUse != instance.session) {
                    sessionToUse.close()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugLlmHelper.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugMlKitHelper.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.util.Log
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Task
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
import com.google.mlkit.vision.common.InputImage
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.DetectedObject
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.ObjectDetection
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.ObjectDetector
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.defaults.ObjectDetectorOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.Text
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognition
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognizer
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.latin.TextRecognizerOptions
import java.lang.StringBuilder

private const val TAG = "DebugMlKitHelper"

/**
 * Un helper pour encapsuler la logique d'appel à ML Kit pour la DebugActivity.
 * Il s'inspire de l'architecture VisionProcessorBase de l'application de démo de Google.
 */
object DebugMlKitHelper {

    // --- Reconnaissance de Texte (OCR) ---

    fun runOcr(context: Context, imageResourceId: Int, onResult: (String) -> Unit) {
        val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(context.resources, imageResourceId)
        val image = InputImage.fromBitmap(bitmap, 0)
        val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(TextRecognizerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS)

        recognizer.process(image)
            .addOnSuccessListener { visionText ->
                val resultText = processOcrResult(visionText)
                onResult(resultText)
                recognizer.close()
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { e ->
                Log.e(TAG, "Text recognition failed", e)
                onResult("Erreur OCR: ${e.message}")
                recognizer.close()
            }
    }

    private fun processOcrResult(texts: Text): String {
        val result = StringBuilder()
        if (texts.textBlocks.isEmpty()) {
            return "Aucun texte détecté."
        }
        for (block in texts.textBlocks) {
            result.append("Block: \"${block.text}\"\n")
            for (line in block.lines) {
                result.append("  Line: \"${line.text}\"\n")
            }
        }
        return result.toString()
    }


    // --- Détection d'Objets ---

    fun runObjectDetection(context: Context, imageResourceId: Int, onResult: (String) -> Unit) {
        val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(context.resources, imageResourceId)
        val image = InputImage.fromBitmap(bitmap, 0)

        // Configure l'ObjectDetector pour détecter et classifier plusieurs objets.
        val options = ObjectDetectorOptions.Builder()
            .setDetectorMode(ObjectDetectorOptions.SINGLE_IMAGE_MODE)
            .enableMultipleObjects()
            .enableClassification()
            .build()
        val objectDetector = ObjectDetection.getClient(options)

        objectDetector.process(image)
            .addOnSuccessListener { detectedObjects ->
                val resultText = processObjectDetectionResult(detectedObjects, bitmap.width, bitmap.height)
                onResult(resultText)
                objectDetector.close()
            }
            .addOnFailureListener { e ->
                Log.e(TAG, "Object detection failed", e)
                onResult("Erreur Détection d'Objets: ${e.message}")
                objectDetector.close()
            }
    }

    private fun processObjectDetectionResult(objects: List<DetectedObject>, width: Int, height: Int): String {
        if (objects.isEmpty()) {
            return "Aucun objet détecté."
        }
        val result = StringBuilder()
        result.append("Dimensions de l'image: ${width}x${height}\n\n")
        for ((index, obj) in objects.withIndex()) {
            result.append("--- Objet ${index + 1} ---\n")
            obj.trackingId?.let {
                result.append("ID de suivi: $it\n")
            }
            result.append("Boîte: ${obj.boundingBox}\n")
            obj.labels.forEach { label ->
                result.append("  Label: ${label.text}\n")
                result.append("  Confiance: %.2f%%\n".format(label.confidence * 100))
            }
        }
        return result.toString()
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugMlKitHelper.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugMlKitMultiHelper.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Task
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
import com.google.mlkit.common.model.LocalModel
import com.google.mlkit.vision.common.InputImage
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.ImageLabel
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.ImageLabeler
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.ImageLabeling
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.custom.CustomImageLabelerOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.defaults.ImageLabelerOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.DetectedObject
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.ObjectDetection
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.defaults.ObjectDetectorOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.Text
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognition
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.japanese.JapaneseTextRecognizerOptions
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.Closeable
import java.lang.StringBuilder
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.coroutines.resume

private const val TAG = "DebugMlKitMultiHelper"

// Structures de données pour un résultat plus riche
data class PerObjectAnalysis(
    val objectLabel: String,
    val confidence: Float,
    val boundingBox: Rect,
    val specialistLabels: Map<String, List<ImageLabel>>
)

data class MultiDetectionResult(
    val formattedText: String,
    val detectedObjects: List<DetectedObject>,
    val ocrText: String = "",
    val globalClassifierResults: Map<String, List<ImageLabel>> = emptyMap(),
    val perObjectAnalyses: List<PerObjectAnalysis> = emptyList()
)

data class SwarmAnalysisResult(
    val reports: List<MultiDetectionResult>,
    val totalLatency: Long
)

/**
 * Orchestrateur pour les processeurs ML Kit et TensorFlow Lite sur une ou plusieurs images.
 */
object DebugMlKitMultiHelper {

    // BOURDON'S FORGE V27: Mise à jour de la signature pour accepter la nouvelle structure PollenImage.
    fun runMultiDetection(
        context: Context,
        pollenImages: List<PollenImage>,
        runOcr: Boolean,
        runObjectDetection: Boolean,
        runDefaultLabeling: Boolean,
        runBirdsClassifier: Boolean,
        runPlantsClassifier: Boolean,
        runInsectsClassifier: Boolean,
        runFoodClassifier: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite0Model: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite1Model: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite2Model: Boolean,
        runMobilenetV1QuantizedModel: Boolean,
        runDeepAnalysis: Boolean,
        onResult: (SwarmAnalysisResult) -> Unit
    ) {
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            val startTime = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
            val aggregatedReports = mutableListOf<MultiDetectionResult>()

            // BOURDON'S FORGE V27: Itération sur les PollenImage et extraction du bitmap haute résolution.
            for ((index, pollenImage) in pollenImages.withIndex()) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Analyse de l'image ${index + 1}/${pollenImages.size} de l'essaim...")
                val singleImageResult = kotlin.coroutines.suspendCoroutine { continuation ->
                    runComplexMultiDetection(
                        context, pollenImage.highResBitmap, runOcr, runObjectDetection, runDefaultLabeling,
                        runBirdsClassifier, runPlantsClassifier, runInsectsClassifier, runFoodClassifier,
                        runEfficientnetLite0Model, runEfficientnetLite1Model, runEfficientnetLite2Model,
                        runMobilenetV1QuantizedModel, runDeepAnalysis
                    ) { result ->
                        continuation.resume(result)
                    }
                }
                aggregatedReports.add(singleImageResult)
            }

            val totalLatency = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - startTime
            Log.d(TAG, "Analyse complète de l'essaim terminée en ${totalLatency}ms.")
            val swarmResult = SwarmAnalysisResult(aggregatedReports, totalLatency)

            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                onResult(swarmResult)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun runComplexMultiDetection(
        context: Context,
        bitmap: Bitmap,
        runOcr: Boolean,
        runObjectDetection: Boolean,
        runDefaultLabeling: Boolean,
        runBirdsClassifier: Boolean,
        runPlantsClassifier: Boolean,
        runInsectsClassifier: Boolean,
        runFoodClassifier: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite0Model: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite1Model: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite2Model: Boolean,
        runMobilenetV1QuantizedModel: Boolean,
        runDeepAnalysis: Boolean,
        onResult: (MultiDetectionResult) -> Unit
    ) {
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            val startTime = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
            val clientsToClose = mutableListOf<Closeable>()
            val image = InputImage.fromBitmap(bitmap, 0)

            var detectedObjects = emptyList<DetectedObject>()
            var ocrText = ""
            val globalClassifierResults = mutableMapOf<String, List<ImageLabel>>()

            try {
                // --- Phase 1: Exécution des modèles ML Kit Standard ---
                val standardTasks = mutableListOf<Task<*>>()

                if (runOcr) {
                    val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(JapaneseTextRecognizerOptions.Builder().build())
                    clientsToClose.add(recognizer)
                    standardTasks.add(recognizer.process(image))
                }

                if (runObjectDetection || runDeepAnalysis) {
                    val objectDetectorOptions = ObjectDetectorOptions.Builder()
                        .setDetectorMode(ObjectDetectorOptions.SINGLE_IMAGE_MODE)
                        .enableMultipleObjects()
                        .enableClassification().build()
                    val objectDetector = ObjectDetection.getClient(objectDetectorOptions)
                    clientsToClose.add(objectDetector)
                    standardTasks.add(objectDetector.process(image))
                }

                val standardClassifiers = getStandardClassifierClients(
                    runDefaultLabeling, runBirdsClassifier, runInsectsClassifier,
                    runPlantsClassifier, runFoodClassifier, runEfficientnetLite0Model,
                    runEfficientnetLite1Model, runEfficientnetLite2Model, runMobilenetV1QuantizedModel
                )
                standardClassifiers.forEach { (_, client) ->
                    clientsToClose.add(client)
                    standardTasks.add(client.process(image))
                }



                // --- Attendre les résultats et les traiter ---
                if (standardTasks.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val standardResults = Tasks.await(Tasks.whenAllSuccess<Any>(standardTasks))
                    var resultIndex = 0

                    if (runOcr) ocrText = processOcrResult(standardResults[resultIndex++] as Text)
                    if (runObjectDetection || runDeepAnalysis) detectedObjects = standardResults[resultIndex++] as List<DetectedObject>

                    standardClassifiers.forEach { (name, _) ->
                        globalClassifierResults[name] = standardResults[resultIndex++] as List<ImageLabel>
                    }
                }

                val globalLatency = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - startTime

                // --- Phase 2: Analyse Ciblée par Objet (Deep Analysis) ---
                var perObjectAnalyses = emptyList<PerObjectAnalysis>()
                var deepAnalysisLatency = 0L

                if (runDeepAnalysis && detectedObjects.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val deepAnalysisStartTime = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
                    perObjectAnalyses = runPerObjectAnalysis(context, bitmap, detectedObjects)
                    deepAnalysisLatency = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - deepAnalysisStartTime
                }

                // --- Phase 3: Formatage final ---
                val finalFormattedText = formatRichResults(globalLatency, ocrText, globalClassifierResults, perObjectAnalyses, deepAnalysisLatency)
                val finalResult = MultiDetectionResult(
                    formattedText = finalFormattedText,
                    detectedObjects = detectedObjects,
                    ocrText = ocrText.replace("Aucun texte détecté.\n", "").trim(),
                    globalClassifierResults = globalClassifierResults,
                    perObjectAnalyses = perObjectAnalyses
                )
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    onResult(finalResult)
                }

            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "L'une des tâches ML a échoué en mode complexe", e)
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    onResult(MultiDetectionResult("Erreur: ${e.message}", emptyList()))
                }
            } finally {
                clientsToClose.forEach { it.close() }
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun runPerObjectAnalysis(
        context: Context,
        bitmap: Bitmap,
        detectedObjects: List<DetectedObject>
    ): List<PerObjectAnalysis> {
        val perObjectAnalyses = mutableListOf<PerObjectAnalysis>()

        val specialistClassifiers = getStandardClassifierClients(true, true, true, true, true, true, true, true, true)
        val inatHelper = InatVisionHelper(context)

        try {
            detectedObjects.forEach { obj ->
                val objLabel = obj.labels.firstOrNull()?.text ?: "unknown"
                val objConfidence = obj.labels.firstOrNull()?.confidence ?: 0f
                val box = obj.boundingBox

                if (box.left < 0 || box.top < 0 || box.right > bitmap.width || box.bottom > bitmap.height || box.width() <= 0 || box.height() <= 0) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Bounding box invalide pour l'analyse par objet: $box")
                    return@forEach
                }

                val croppedBitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmap, box.left, box.top, box.width(), box.height())
                val croppedImage = InputImage.fromBitmap(croppedBitmap, 0)
                val specialistTasks = specialistClassifiers.map { (_, client) -> client.process(croppedImage) }

                val specialistResultsRaw = Tasks.await(Tasks.whenAllSuccess<List<ImageLabel>>(specialistTasks))
                val specialistResultsMap = specialistClassifiers.map { it.first }.zip(specialistResultsRaw).toMap().toMutableMap()

                val inatObjectResults = inatHelper.classify(croppedBitmap)
                if(inatObjectResults.isNotEmpty()) {
                    specialistResultsMap["iNaturalist"] = inatObjectResults
                }

                perObjectAnalyses.add(PerObjectAnalysis(objLabel, objConfidence, box, specialistResultsMap))
            }
        } finally {
            specialistClassifiers.forEach { (_, client) -> client.close() }
            inatHelper.close()
        }

        return perObjectAnalyses
    }

    private fun getStandardClassifierClients(
        runDefault: Boolean, runBirds: Boolean, runInsects: Boolean,
        runPlants: Boolean, runFood: Boolean, runEfficientnet: Boolean,
        runEfficientnetLite1: Boolean, runEfficientnetLite2: Boolean, runMobilenetV1Quantized: Boolean
    ): List<Pair<String, ImageLabeler>> {
        val clients = mutableListOf<Pair<String, ImageLabeler>>()
        if (runDefault) clients.add("Défaut" to ImageLabeling.getClient(ImageLabelerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS))
        if (runBirds) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-birds-v1-v3.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("Oiseaux" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runInsects) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-insects-v1-v3.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("Insectes" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runPlants) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-plants-v1-v3.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("Plantes" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runFood) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-food-v1-v1.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("Nourriture" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runEfficientnet) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("efficientnet_lite0.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("EfficientNet-Lite0" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runEfficientnetLite1) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("efficientnet_lite1_int8_2.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("EfficientNet-Lite1" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runEfficientnetLite2) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("efficientnet_lite2_int8_2.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("EfficientNet-Lite2" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        if (runMobilenetV1Quantized) {
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath("mobilenet_v1_1.0_224_quantized_1_metadata_1.tflite").build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            clients.add("MobileNetV1-Quantized" to ImageLabeling.getClient(options))
        }
        return clients
    }

    private fun formatRichResults(
        globalLatency: Long,
        ocrText: String,
        globalResults: Map<String, List<ImageLabel>>,
        perObjectResults: List<PerObjectAnalysis>,
        deepLatency: Long
    ): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("--- ANALYSE GLOBALE (en ${globalLatency}ms) ---\n")
        if (ocrText.isNotBlank() && ocrText != "Aucun texte détecté.\n") sb.append("OCR: $ocrText")
        globalResults.forEach { (classifier, labels) ->
            if (labels.isNotEmpty()) {
                sb.append("Classification ($classifier):\n")
                sb.append(processLabelingResult(labels.take(3)))
            }
        }
        sb.append("\n")
        if (perObjectResults.isNotEmpty()) {
            sb.append("--- ANALYSE APPROFONDIE PAR OBJET (en ${deepLatency}ms) ---\n")
            perObjectResults.forEachIndexed { index, analysis ->
                sb.append("--- Objet ${index + 1}: ${analysis.objectLabel} (${String.format("%.0f%%", analysis.confidence * 100)}) ---\n")
                analysis.specialistLabels.forEach { (classifier, labels) ->
                    if (labels.isNotEmpty()) {
                        sb.append("  > Avis Spécialiste ($classifier): ")
                        val topLabels = labels.take(2)
                            .joinToString(", ") { "${it.text} (${String.format("%.0f%%", it.confidence * 100)})" }
                        sb.append(topLabels)
                        sb.append("\n")
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    private fun processOcrResult(texts: Text): String {
        val result = StringBuilder()
        if (texts.textBlocks.isEmpty()) return "Aucun texte détecté.\n"
        texts.textBlocks.forEach { result.append("\"${it.text}\" ") }
        return result.toString().trim() + "\n"
    }

    private fun processObjectDetectionResult(objects: List<DetectedObject>): String {
        if (objects.isEmpty()) return "Aucun objet détecté.\n"
        val result = StringBuilder()
        objects.forEachIndexed { index, obj ->
            obj.labels.firstOrNull()?.let { label ->
                result.append("- ${label.text} (${String.format("%.0f%%", label.confidence * 100)})\n")
            }
        }
        return result.toString()
    }

    private fun processLabelingResult(labels: List<ImageLabel>): String {
        if (labels.isEmpty()) return "Aucune étiquette détectée.\n"
        val result = StringBuilder()
        labels.forEach { label ->
            result.append("- ${label.text} (${String.format("%.0f%%", label.confidence * 100)})\n")
        }
        return result.toString()
    }
}

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugModelAdapter.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.content.Context
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.ProgressBar
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.core.view.isVisible
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Model
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.ModelDownloadStatus
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.ModelDownloadStatusType
import java.util.Locale

class DebugModelAdapter(
    private val context: Context,
    private var models: List<Model>,
    private var modelStates: Map<String, ModelDownloadStatus>,
    private val onDownloadClick: (Model) -> Unit,
    private val onDeleteClick: (Model) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<DebugModelAdapter.ModelViewHolder>() {

    data class ProgressPayload(val status: ModelDownloadStatus)

    inner class ModelViewHolder(itemView: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(itemView) {
        private val modelNameTextView: TextView = itemView.findViewById(R.id.model_name_textview)
        private val modelProgressBar: ProgressBar = itemView.findViewById(R.id.model_progress_bar)
        private val modelStatusTextView: TextView = itemView.findViewById(R.id.model_status_textview)
        private val modelDownloadButton: Button = itemView.findViewById(R.id.model_download_button)
        private val modelDeleteButton: Button = itemView.findViewById(R.id.model_delete_button)

        fun bind(model: Model, status: ModelDownloadStatus) {
            modelNameTextView.text = model.name
            modelDownloadButton.setOnClickListener { onDownloadClick(model) }
            modelDeleteButton.setOnClickListener { onDeleteClick(model) }
            updateUI(status)
        }

        fun updateProgress(status: ModelDownloadStatus){
            updateUI(status)
        }

        private fun updateUI(status: ModelDownloadStatus) {
            val progressPercent = if (status.totalBytes > 0) (status.receivedBytes * 100 / status.totalBytes).toInt() else 0

            when (status.status) {
                ModelDownloadStatusType.IN_PROGRESS -> {
                    modelProgressBar.isVisible = true
                    modelProgressBar.isIndeterminate = false
                    modelProgressBar.progress = progressPercent

                    val receivedMb = status.receivedBytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
                    val totalMb = status.totalBytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
                    val speedKbps = status.bytesPerSecond / 1024.0

                    // BOURDON'S FIX: Construit la chaîne de statut avec le temps restant.
                    var statusText = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f/%.1f MB (%d%%) - %.1f KB/s", receivedMb, totalMb, progressPercent, speedKbps)
                    if (status.remainingMs > 0) {
                        statusText += " - ${formatRemainingTime(status.remainingMs)}"
                    }
                    modelStatusTextView.text = statusText

                    modelDownloadButton.isEnabled = false
                    modelDeleteButton.isVisible = false
                }
                ModelDownloadStatusType.UNZIPPING -> {
                    modelProgressBar.isVisible = true
                    modelProgressBar.isIndeterminate = true
                    modelStatusTextView.text = context.getString(R.string.status_unzipping)
                    modelDownloadButton.isEnabled = false
                    modelDeleteButton.isVisible = false
                }
                ModelDownloadStatusType.SUCCEEDED -> {
                    modelProgressBar.isVisible = false
                    modelStatusTextView.text = context.getString(R.string.status_ready)
                    modelDownloadButton.isEnabled = false
                    modelDeleteButton.isVisible = true
                }
                else -> {
                    modelProgressBar.isVisible = false
                    modelStatusTextView.text = if (status.status == ModelDownloadStatusType.FAILED) context.getString(R.string.status_error) else context.getString(R.string.status_not_downloaded)
                    modelDownloadButton.isEnabled = true
                    modelDeleteButton.isVisible = false
                }
            }
        }

        // BOURDON'S ADDITION: Fonction utilitaire pour formater les millisecondes.
        private fun formatRemainingTime(millis: Long): String {
            val seconds = millis / 1000
            val minutes = seconds / 60
            val remainingSeconds = seconds % 60
            return if (minutes > 0) {
                String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%dm %ds restants", minutes, remainingSeconds)
            } else {
                String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%ds restant", remainingSeconds)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ModelViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context).inflate(R.layout.item_debug_model, parent, false)
        return ModelViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ModelViewHolder, position: Int, payloads: MutableList<Any>) {
        if (payloads.isEmpty()) {
            super.onBindViewHolder(holder, position, payloads)
        } else {
            payloads.forEach { payload ->
                if (payload is ProgressPayload) {
                    holder.updateProgress(payload.status)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = models.size

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ModelViewHolder, position: Int) {
        val model = models[position]
        val status = modelStates[model.name] ?: ModelDownloadStatus(ModelDownloadStatusType.NOT_DOWNLOADED)
        holder.bind(model, status)
    }

    fun updateModelState(modelName: String, status: ModelDownloadStatus) {
        val index = models.indexOfFirst { it.name == modelName }
        if (index != -1) {
            modelStates.toMutableMap()[modelName] = status
            notifyItemChanged(index, ProgressPayload(status))
        }
    }

    fun setAllStates(newStates: Map<String, ModelDownloadStatus>) {
        this.modelStates = newStates
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugModelAdapter.kt ---

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugTranslationHelper.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.util.Log
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
import com.google.mlkit.common.model.DownloadConditions
import com.google.mlkit.nl.languageid.LanguageIdentification
import com.google.mlkit.nl.translate.TranslateLanguage
import com.google.mlkit.nl.translate.Translation
import com.google.mlkit.nl.translate.Translator
import com.google.mlkit.nl.translate.TranslatorOptions
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.coroutines.resume
import kotlin.coroutines.suspendCoroutine

/**
 * Un helper pour encapsuler la logique de traduction de ML Kit pour la DebugActivity.
 */
object DebugTranslationHelper {

    private const val TAG = "DebugTranslationHelper"

    private var translator: Translator? = null
    private var lastOptions: TranslatorOptions? = null
    private val languageIdentifier = LanguageIdentification.getClient()

    /**
     * BOURDON'S FIX: Transformé en fonction suspendue pour un flux asynchrone propre.
     * Ne retourne que le résultat final ou une erreur, sans statut intermédiaire.
     */
    suspend fun translate(textToTranslate: String, targetLanguage: String): String {
        if (textToTranslate.isBlank()) {
            return ""
        }

        return try {
            val languageCode = Tasks.await(languageIdentifier.identifyLanguage(textToTranslate))
            if (languageCode == "und") {
                return "[Erreur: Langue non détectée]"
            }

            if (languageCode == targetLanguage) {
                val langName = Locale.forLanguageTag(languageCode).displayName
                return "[Texte déjà en ${langName}]"
            }

            Log.d(TAG, "Langue détectée: $languageCode, Cible: $targetLanguage")
            executeTranslation(textToTranslate, languageCode, targetLanguage)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "L'identification ou la traduction a échoué", e)
            "[Erreur: ${e.message}]"
        }
    }

    private suspend fun executeTranslation(textToTranslate: String, sourceLanguage: String, targetLanguage: String): String {
        val options = TranslatorOptions.Builder()
            .setSourceLanguage(sourceLanguage)
            .setTargetLanguage(targetLanguage)
            .build()

        // Réutilise ou crée un nouveau traducteur si les options ont changé
        if (lastOptions != options) {
            closeTranslator()
            lastOptions = options
            translator = Translation.getClient(options)
        }
        val localTranslator = translator ?: return "[Erreur: Client de traduction non initialisé]"

        // Télécharge le modèle si nécessaire
        val conditions = DownloadConditions.Builder().requireWifi().build()
        Tasks.await(localTranslator.downloadModelIfNeeded(conditions))
        Log.d(TAG, "Modèle de traduction prêt.")

        // Effectue la traduction
        return Tasks.await(localTranslator.translate(textToTranslate))
    }


    fun closeTranslator() {
        translator?.close()
        translator = null
        lastOptions = null
        Log.d(TAG, "Client de traduction fermé.")
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DebugTranslationHelper.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/DownloadManagerKikko.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.Data
import androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_FILE_NAME
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_IS_ZIP
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_NAME
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_TOTAL_BYTES
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_UNZIPPED_DIR
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_URL
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Model

object DownloadManagerKikko {

    fun startDownload(context: Context, model: Model) {
        val workManager = WorkManager.getInstance(context.applicationContext)

        val inputData = Data.Builder()
            .putString(KEY_MODEL_NAME, model.name)
            .putString(KEY_MODEL_URL, model.url)
            .putString(KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_FILE_NAME, model.downloadFileName)
            .putLong(KEY_MODEL_TOTAL_BYTES, model.sizeInBytes)
            .putBoolean(KEY_MODEL_IS_ZIP, model.isZip)
            .putString(KEY_MODEL_UNZIPPED_DIR, model.unzipDir)
            .build()

        val downloadWorkRequest = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<KikkoDownloadWorker>()
            .setInputData(inputData)
            .addTag(model.name) // On utilise le nom comme tag unique
            .build()

        workManager.enqueueUniqueWork(
            model.name,
            ExistingWorkPolicy.REPLACE,
            downloadWorkRequest
        )
    }
}

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

/**
 * Représente une seule entrée dans le journal de forge (Journal de Bord).
 * Capture l'intégralité d'une transaction avec un modèle LLM pour analyse et débogage.
 */
@Serializable
data class InferenceLog(
    val inferenceId: String,
    val timestamp: String,
    val useCase: String,
    val model: ModelInfo,
    val prompt: String, // AJOUTÉ : Le prompt exact envoyé au modèle.
    val rawResponse: String, // AJOUTÉ : La réponse brute complète reçue.
    val metadata: InferenceMetadata
)

/**
 * Contient les informations sur le modèle utilisé pour l'inférence.
 */
@Serializable
data class ModelInfo(
    val name: String,
    val configuration: ModelConfiguration
)

/**
 * Contient les paramètres de configuration clés utilisés pour cette inférence spécifique.
 */
@Serializable
data class ModelConfiguration(
    val temperature: Float,
    val topK: Int,
    val maxTokens: Int,
    val accelerator: String
)

/**
 * Contient les métadonnées de performance de l'inférence.
 */
@Serializable
data class InferenceMetadata(
    val latencyMs: Long,
    val tokensPerSecond: Float
)

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/InferenceLogger.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import kotlinx.serialization.encodeToString
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

/**
 * Un logger singleton en mémoire pour conserver une trace de toutes les inférences LLM
 * effectuées pendant une session de débogage.
 *
 * NOTE : Ce logger est volatile. Son contenu est perdu lorsque l'application est fermée.
 */
object InferenceLogger {

    // Configure l'encodeur JSON pour une sortie lisible (pretty print).
    private val json = Json { prettyPrint = true }

    // La liste privée et mutable qui stocke nos entrées de journal.
    private val logEntries = mutableListOf<InferenceLog>()

    /**
     * Ajoute une nouvelle entrée de journal à la liste de manière thread-safe.
     * @param entry L'objet InferenceLog à ajouter.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun add(entry: InferenceLog) {
        logEntries.add(entry)
    }

    /**
     * Vide complètement le journal de manière thread-safe.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun clear() {
        logEntries.clear()
    }

    /**
     * Renvoie une copie immuable de toutes les entrées du journal.
     * @return Une List<InferenceLog> des entrées actuelles.
     */
    fun getLogs(): List<InferenceLog> {
        return logEntries.toList()
    }

    /**
     * Compte le nombre d'entrées actuellement dans le journal.
     * @return Le nombre d'inférences enregistrées.
     */
    fun count(): Int {
        return logEntries.size
    }

    /**
     * Sérialise l'ensemble du journal en une chaîne de caractères JSON formatée.
     * @return Une chaîne JSON représentant la liste des entrées, ou un message d'erreur.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun exportToJson(): String {
        if (logEntries.isEmpty()) {
            return "{\"status\": \"Le journal de forge est vide.\"}"
        }
        return try {
            json.encodeToString(logEntries)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            "{\"error\": \"Échec de la sérialisation du journal en JSON.\", \"details\": \"${e.message}\"}"
        }
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/InferenceLogger.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/KikkoDownloadWorker.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.Manifest
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import androidx.localbroadcastmanager.content.LocalBroadcastManager
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.Data
import androidx.work.ForegroundInfo
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_FILE_NAME
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_REMAINING_MS
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_IS_ZIP
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_NAME
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_START_UNZIPPING
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_TOTAL_BYTES
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_UNZIPPED_DIR
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.KEY_MODEL_URL
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry
import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream

class KikkoDownloadWorker(val context: Context, params: WorkerParameters) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_PROGRESS_UPDATE = "com.google.edge.kikko.debug.PROGRESS_UPDATE"
        const val EXTRA_MODEL_NAME = "EXTRA_MODEL_NAME"
        const val EXTRA_RECEIVED_BYTES = "EXTRA_RECEIVED_BYTES"
        const val EXTRA_TOTAL_BYTES = "EXTRA_TOTAL_BYTES"
        const val EXTRA_DOWNLOAD_SPEED_BPS = "EXTRA_DOWNLOAD_SPEED_BPS"
        const val NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID = "kikko_download_channel"
    }

    private val TAG = "KikkoDownloadWorker"
    private val notificationManager = NotificationManagerCompat.from(applicationContext)

    init {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID,
                "Téléchargements de modèles Kikko",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            )
            notificationManager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val modelName = inputData.getString(KEY_MODEL_NAME) ?: return Result.failure()
        val fileUrl = inputData.getString(KEY_MODEL_URL) ?: return Result.failure()
        val fileName = inputData.getString(KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_FILE_NAME) ?: return Result.failure()
        val estimatedTotalBytes = inputData.getLong(KEY_MODEL_TOTAL_BYTES, 0L)
        val isZip = inputData.getBoolean(KEY_MODEL_IS_ZIP, false)
        val unzipDir = inputData.getString(KEY_MODEL_UNZIPPED_DIR)

        val modelBaseDir = File(applicationContext.getExternalFilesDir(null), "debug_models")
        if (!modelBaseDir.exists()) modelBaseDir.mkdirs()
        val modelDir = File(modelBaseDir, modelName)
        if (!modelDir.exists()) modelDir.mkdirs()

        val outputFile = File(modelDir, fileName)

        try {
            setForeground(createForegroundInfo("Démarrage du téléchargement pour $modelName", 0))
            downloadFile(fileUrl, outputFile, estimatedTotalBytes, modelName)

            if (isZip && unzipDir != null) {
                setForeground(createForegroundInfo("Décompression de $modelName...", 0, true))
                setProgress(Data.Builder().putBoolean(KEY_MODEL_START_UNZIPPING, true).build())
                unzip(outputFile, modelDir)
                outputFile.delete()
            }
            return Result.success()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            if (e is CancellationException || e is InterruptedException) {
                Log.i(TAG, "Download cancelled for $modelName")
                // WorkManager will handle the CANCELLED state.
            } else {
                Log.e(TAG, "Download or Unzip failed for $modelName", e)
                if(outputFile.exists()) outputFile.delete()
                return Result.failure()
            }
            // In case of cancellation, we let the worker finish without a specific result.
            // Returning success to prevent WorkManager from retrying a cancelled job.
            return Result.success()
        }
    }

    private suspend fun downloadFile(downloadUrl: String, outputFile: File, estimatedTotalBytes: Long, modelName: String) {
        val url = URL(downloadUrl)
        val connection = url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
        connection.connect()

        if (connection.responseCode != HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
            throw IOException("Server returned HTTP ${connection.responseCode} ${connection.responseMessage}")
        }

        val actualTotalBytes = if (connection.contentLength > 0) connection.contentLength.toLong() else estimatedTotalBytes

        val inputStream = connection.inputStream
        val outputStream = FileOutputStream(outputFile)
        val buffer = ByteArray(8 * 1024)
        var bytesCopied: Long = 0
        var bytesRead: Int
        var lastReportTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        var lastReportedBytes: Long = 0
        val bytesReadSizeBuffer: MutableList<Long> = mutableListOf()
        val bytesReadLatencyBuffer: MutableList<Long> = mutableListOf()

        try {
            while (inputStream.read(buffer).also { bytesRead = it } != -1) {
                if (isStopped) throw InterruptedException("Download cancelled")
                outputStream.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead)
                bytesCopied += bytesRead

                val currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                if (currentTime - lastReportTime >= 500) { // Update notification less frequently
                    val timeDelta = (currentTime - lastReportTime)
                    val bytesDelta = bytesCopied - lastReportedBytes
                    val speedBps = if (timeDelta > 0) (bytesDelta * 1000 / timeDelta) else 0L

                    var remainingMs = -1L
                    bytesReadSizeBuffer.add(bytesDelta)
                    if (bytesReadSizeBuffer.size > 5) bytesReadSizeBuffer.removeAt(0)
                    bytesReadLatencyBuffer.add(timeDelta)
                    if (bytesReadLatencyBuffer.size > 5) bytesReadLatencyBuffer.removeAt(0)

                    val totalRecentBytes = bytesReadSizeBuffer.sum()
                    val totalRecentTime = bytesReadLatencyBuffer.sum()
                    if (totalRecentTime > 0 && totalRecentBytes > 0) {
                        val avgSpeedBps = totalRecentBytes * 1000 / totalRecentTime
                        if (avgSpeedBps > 0) {
                            remainingMs = (actualTotalBytes - bytesCopied) * 1000 / avgSpeedBps
                        }
                    }

                    val progressPercent = if (actualTotalBytes > 0) (bytesCopied * 100 / actualTotalBytes).toInt() else 0
                    val progressText = "Téléchargement de $modelName ($progressPercent%)"
                    setForeground(createForegroundInfo(progressText, progressPercent))

                    val intent = Intent(ACTION_PROGRESS_UPDATE).apply {
                        putExtra(EXTRA_MODEL_NAME, modelName)
                        putExtra(EXTRA_RECEIVED_BYTES, bytesCopied)
                        putExtra(EXTRA_TOTAL_BYTES, actualTotalBytes)
                        putExtra(EXTRA_DOWNLOAD_SPEED_BPS, speedBps)
                        putExtra(KEY_MODEL_DOWNLOAD_REMAINING_MS, remainingMs)
                    }
                    LocalBroadcastManager.getInstance(context).sendBroadcast(intent)

                    lastReportTime = currentTime
                    lastReportedBytes = bytesCopied
                    delay(50)
                }
            }
        } finally {
            outputStream.close()
            inputStream.close()
        }
    }

    private fun unzip(zipFile: File, targetParentDirectory: File) {
        Log.d(TAG, "Unzipping ${zipFile.name} into ${targetParentDirectory.name}")
        val tempUnzipDir = File(zipFile.parentFile, "temp_unzip_${zipFile.nameWithoutExtension}")
        if (tempUnzipDir.exists()) tempUnzipDir.deleteRecursively()
        tempUnzipDir.mkdirs()

        try {
            ZipInputStream(BufferedInputStream(FileInputStream(zipFile))).use { zis ->
                var entry: ZipEntry? = zis.nextEntry
                while (entry != null) {
                    if (isStopped) throw InterruptedException("Unzip cancelled")

                    val newFile = File(tempUnzipDir, entry.name)
                    if (!newFile.canonicalPath.startsWith(tempUnzipDir.canonicalPath + File.separator)) {
                        throw IOException("Zip Slip vulnerability detected!")
                    }

                    if (entry.isDirectory) {
                        if (!newFile.isDirectory && !newFile.mkdirs()) {
                            throw IOException("Failed to create directory ${newFile.absolutePath}")
                        }
                    } else {
                        newFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()
                        FileOutputStream(newFile).use { fos -> zis.copyTo(fos) }
                    }
                    zis.closeEntry()
                    entry = zis.nextEntry
                }
            }

            val filesInTemp = tempUnzipDir.listFiles()
            if (filesInTemp != null && filesInTemp.size == 1 && filesInTemp[0].isDirectory) {
                val singleRootFolder = filesInTemp[0]
                singleRootFolder.listFiles()?.forEach { fileToMove ->
                    fileToMove.renameTo(File(targetParentDirectory, fileToMove.name))
                }
            } else {
                filesInTemp?.forEach { fileToMove ->
                    fileToMove.renameTo(File(targetParentDirectory, fileToMove.name))
                }
            }
        } finally {
            tempUnzipDir.deleteRecursively()
        }
    }

    override suspend fun getForegroundInfo(): ForegroundInfo {
        return createForegroundInfo("Préparation du téléchargement...", 0)
    }

    private fun createForegroundInfo(text: String, progress: Int, indeterminate: Boolean = false): ForegroundInfo {
        val notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(applicationContext, NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("Forge de la Ruche Kikko")
            .setContentText(text)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground) // Assurez-vous d'avoir une icône ici
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setProgress(100, progress, indeterminate)
            .build()

        return ForegroundInfo(id.hashCode(), notification)
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/KikkoDownloadWorker.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageButton
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R
import java.io.File

class LocalModelAdapter(
    private var models: MutableList<File>,
    private val onDeleteClick: (File) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<LocalModelAdapter.ViewHolder>() {

    class ViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
        val modelName: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.local_model_name_textview)
        val deleteButton: ImageButton = view.findViewById(R.id.local_model_delete_button)
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            .inflate(R.layout.item_local_model, parent, false)
        return ViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        val modelFile = models[position]
        holder.modelName.text = modelFile.name
        holder.deleteButton.setOnClickListener {
            onDeleteClick(modelFile)
        }
    }

    override fun getItemCount() = models.size

    fun updateModels(newModels: List<File>) {
        models.clear()
        models.addAll(newModels)
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/LocalModelAdapter.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/LocalModelSelectorActivity.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R

class LocalModelSelectorActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var selectModelButton: Button
    private lateinit var selectedModelPathTextView: TextView
    private lateinit var confirmModelButton: Button

    private var selectedModelUri: Uri? = null

    private val selectFileLauncher = registerForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.StartActivityForResult()) { result ->
        if (result.resultCode == Activity.RESULT_OK) {
            result.data?.data?.also { uri ->
                selectedModelUri = uri
                // Persist permission to read the file across reboots
                contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(uri, Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                selectedModelPathTextView.text = uri.path
                confirmModelButton.isEnabled = true
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_local_model_selector)

        selectModelButton = findViewById(R.id.select_model_button)
        selectedModelPathTextView = findViewById(R.id.selected_model_path_textview)
        confirmModelButton = findViewById(R.id.confirm_model_button)

        selectModelButton.setOnClickListener {
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT).apply {
                addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
                type = "*/*" // Allow all file types, user should select a .task
            }
            selectFileLauncher.launch(intent)
        }

        confirmModelButton.setOnClickListener {
            selectedModelUri?.let {
                val resultIntent = Intent()
                resultIntent.data = it
                setResult(Activity.RESULT_OK, resultIntent)
                finish()
            }
        }
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/debug/LocalModelSelectorActivity.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.debug

import android.graphics.Bitmap

/**
 * BOURDON'S FORGE V27: Nouvelle structure de données pour gérer les deux résolutions d'image.
 *
 * Cette data class encapsule les différentes versions d'une image capturée ("pollen")
 * pour optimiser le flux de travail de la Ruche.
 *
 * @property highResBitmap Bitmap haute résolution (max 1024px) destiné aux Abeilles Spécialistes (ML Kit/TFLite) pour une analyse précise.
 * @property lowResBitmap Bitmap basse résolution (max 512px) destiné à la Reine IA (Gemma 3n) pour une inférence visuelle rapide.
 * @property highResTempPath Chemin d'accès vers le fichier temporaire de l'image haute résolution, utilisé pour la sauvegarde finale de la carte.
 */
data class PollenImage(
    val highResBitmap: Bitmap,
    val lowResBitmap: Bitmap,
    val highResTempPath: String
)
