        if (uris.isNotEmpty()) handleImageUris(uris)
    }
    private val takePhotoLauncher = registerForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.TakePicture()) { success ->
        if (success) tempPhotoUri?.let { handleImageUris(listOf(it)) }
    }
    private val requestCameraPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()) { isGranted ->
        if (isGranted) startCameraIntent()
        else Toast.makeText(this, "Permission Caméra refusée.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_forge)
        title = "Atelier de la Forge"

        bindViews()
        setupRecyclerView()
        setupListeners()
        setupModelSpinner()
        setupObservers()
    }

    private fun bindViews() {
        modelSpinner = findViewById(R.id.forge_model_spinner)
        acceleratorRadioGroup = findViewById(R.id.forge_accelerator_radiogroup)
        chooseImageButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_choose_image)
        takePhotoButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_take_photo)
        pollenPreviewRecyclerView = findViewById(R.id.forge_pollen_preview_recyclerview)
        pollenPlaceholderTextView = findViewById(R.id.forge_pollen_placeholder_text)
        promptEditText = findViewById(R.id.forge_prompt_edittext)
        runIdentificationButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_run_button)
        statusTextView = findViewById(R.id.forge_status_textview)
        runMlkitButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_run_mlkit)
        mlkitReportHeader = findViewById(R.id.forge_mlkit_report_header)
        mlkitReportTextView = findViewById(R.id.forge_mlkit_report_textview)

        // Switches
        switchOcr = findViewById(R.id.switch_ocr)
        switchObjectDetection = findViewById(R.id.switch_object_detection)
        switchDefaultLabeling = findViewById(R.id.switch_default_labeling)
        switchBirdsClassifier = findViewById(R.id.switch_birds_classifier)
        switchInsectsClassifier = findViewById(R.id.switch_insects_classifier)
        switchPlantsClassifier = findViewById(R.id.switch_plants_classifier)
        switchFoodClassifier = findViewById(R.id.switch_food_classifier)
        switchMobilenetV1Quantized = findViewById(R.id.switch_mobilenet_v1_quantized)
        switchEfficientnet = findViewById(R.id.switch_efficientnet)
        switchEfficientnetLite1 = findViewById(R.id.switch_efficientnet_lite1)
        switchEfficientnetLite2 = findViewById(R.id.switch_efficientnet_lite2)
        switchDeepObjectAnalysis = findViewById(R.id.switch_deep_object_analysis)


        // Carte résultat
        resultCardContainer = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_result_container)
        cardSpecificName = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_specificName)
        cardDeckName = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_deckName)
        cardReasoning = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_reasoning)
        cardDescription = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_description)
        cardStats = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_stats)
        cardAllergensTitle = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_allergens_title)
        cardAllergens = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_allergens)
        cardIngredientsTitle = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_ingredients_title)
        cardIngredients = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_ingredients)
        cardQuizContainer = findViewById(R.id.forge_card_quiz_container)

        // Boutons d'action
        actionButtonsContainer = findViewById(R.id.forge_action_buttons_container)
        generateDescButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_generate_desc)
        generateStatsButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_generate_stats)
        generateQuizButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_generate_quiz)
        finishButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_finish)
        discardButton = findViewById(R.id.forge_button_discard)
    }

    private fun setupRecyclerView() {
        pollenAdapter = PollenPreviewAdapter(mutableListOf()) { viewModel.removePollenImage(it) }
        pollenPreviewRecyclerView.apply {
            layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(this@ForgeActivity, LinearLayoutManager.HORIZONTAL, false)
            adapter = pollenAdapter
        }
    }

    private fun setupListeners() {
        runIdentificationButton.setOnClickListener { viewModel.runIdentification(promptEditText.text.toString()) }
        acceleratorRadioGroup.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, checkedId ->
            viewModel.onAcceleratorSelected(if (checkedId == R.id.forge_cpu_radio) Accelerator.CPU.label else Accelerator.GPU.label)
        }
        promptEditText.addTextChangedListener {
            // Button state is now managed inside the observer
        }
        chooseImageButton.setOnClickListener {
            pickMultipleImagesLauncher.launch(PickVisualMediaRequest(ActivityResultContracts.PickVisualMedia.ImageOnly))
        }
        takePhotoButton.setOnClickListener {
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.CAMERA) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) startCameraIntent()
            else requestCameraPermissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
        }
        runMlkitButton.setOnClickListener {
            viewModel.runMlKitAnalysis(mapOf(
                ForgeMlKitHelper.OCR to switchOcr.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.OBJECT_DETECTION to switchObjectDetection.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.DEFAULT_LABELING to switchDefaultLabeling.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.BIRDS_CLASSIFIER to switchBirdsClassifier.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.INSECTS_CLASSIFIER to switchInsectsClassifier.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.PLANTS_CLASSIFIER to switchPlantsClassifier.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.FOOD_CLASSIFIER to switchFoodClassifier.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.MOBILENET_V1 to switchMobilenetV1Quantized.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.EFFICIENTNET_LITE0 to switchEfficientnet.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.EFFICIENTNET_LITE1 to switchEfficientnetLite1.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.EFFICIENTNET_LITE2 to switchEfficientnetLite2.isChecked,
                ForgeMlKitHelper.DEEP_OBJECT_ANALYSIS to switchDeepObjectAnalysis.isChecked
            ))
        }
        mlkitReportHeader.setOnClickListener {
            mlkitReportTextView.isVisible = !mlkitReportTextView.isVisible
        }
        generateDescButton.setOnClickListener { viewModel.generateDescription() }
        generateStatsButton.setOnClickListener { viewModel.generateStats() }
        generateQuizButton.setOnClickListener { viewModel.generateQuiz() }
        finishButton.setOnClickListener { finish() }
        discardButton.setOnClickListener { viewModel.discardForgedResult() }
    }

    private fun startCameraIntent() {
        createTempPhotoUri().let { uri ->
            tempPhotoUri = uri
            takePhotoLauncher.launch(uri)
        }
    }

    private fun setupObservers() {
        lifecycleScope.launch {
            repeatOnLifecycle(Lifecycle.State.STARTED) {
                viewModel.uiState.collect { state ->
                    val isBusy = state.isInferring || state.isModelLoading || state.isAnalyzingMlKit

                    if (state.isInferring && state.streamingCharCount > 0) {
                        statusTextView.text = "${state.statusText} (${state.streamingCharCount} caractères)"
                    } else {
                        statusTextView.text = state.statusText
                    }

                    val modelNames = state.availableModels.map { it.name }
                    val currentAdapter = modelSpinner.adapter as? ArrayAdapter<String>
                    if (currentAdapter == null || currentAdapter.count != modelNames.size || (0 until currentAdapter.count).any { currentAdapter.getItem(it) != modelNames[it] }) {
                        val adapter = ArrayAdapter(this@ForgeActivity, android.R.layout.simple_spinner_item, modelNames)
                        adapter.setDropDownViewResource(android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item)
                        modelSpinner.adapter = adapter
                    }

                    state.selectedModel?.let { selectedModel ->
                        val position = modelNames.indexOf(selectedModel.name)
                        if (position >= 0 && modelSpinner.selectedItemPosition != position) {
                            modelSpinner.setSelection(position)
                        }
                    }

                    val hasPollen = state.pollenImages.isNotEmpty()
                    val hasPrompt = promptEditText.text?.isNotBlank() == true
                    runMlkitButton.isEnabled = !isBusy && hasPollen
                    runIdentificationButton.isEnabled = !isBusy && hasPollen && hasPrompt && !state.isModelLoading

                    pollenAdapter.updateImages(state.pollenImages)
                    pollenPlaceholderTextView.isVisible = !hasPollen
                    pollenPreviewRecyclerView.isVisible = hasPollen

                    if (state.generatedPrompt.isNotBlank() && promptEditText.text.toString() != state.generatedPrompt) {
                        promptEditText.setText(state.generatedPrompt)
                    }
                    mlkitReportTextView.text = state.mlKitReport
                    mlkitReportHeader.isVisible = state.mlKitReport.isNotBlank()


                    val card = state.forgedCard
                    resultCardContainer.isVisible = card != null
                    actionButtonsContainer.isVisible = card != null

                    if (card != null) {
                        cardSpecificName.text = card.specificName
                        cardDeckName.text = card.deckName
                        cardReasoning.text = "Visual: ${card.reasoning.visualAnalysis}\nCorrelation: ${card.reasoning.evidenceCorrelation}"

                        cardDescription.isVisible = !card.description.isNullOrBlank()
                        cardDescription.text = card.description

                        cardStats.isVisible = card.stats != null
                        cardStats.text = card.stats?.items?.entries?.joinToString("\n") { (key, value) -> "$key: $value" } ?: ""

                        val allergensList = card.allergens
                        if (!allergensList.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                            cardAllergensTitle.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                            cardAllergens.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                            cardAllergensTitle.text = "Allergènes (${allergensList.size}):"
                            cardAllergens.text = allergensList.joinToString(", ")
                        } else {
                            cardAllergensTitle.visibility = View.GONE
                            cardAllergens.visibility = View.GONE
                        }

                        val ingredientsList = card.ingredients
                        if (!ingredientsList.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                            cardIngredientsTitle.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                            cardIngredients.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                            cardIngredientsTitle.text = "Ingrédients (${ingredientsList.size}):"
                            cardIngredients.text = ingredientsList.joinToString(", ")
                        } else {
                            cardIngredientsTitle.visibility = View.GONE
                            cardIngredients.visibility = View.GONE
                        }

                        // BOURDON'S FIX: Check for null before populating the quiz container.
                        val quizList = card.quiz
                        cardQuizContainer.isVisible = !quizList.isNullOrEmpty()
                        if (!quizList.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                            populateQuizContainer(cardQuizContainer, quizList)
                        }


                        generateDescButton.isEnabled = !isBusy
                        generateDescButton.isVisible = state.isIdentificationReady && card.description == null

                        generateStatsButton.isEnabled = !isBusy
                        generateStatsButton.isVisible = card.description != null && card.stats == null

                        generateQuizButton.isEnabled = !isBusy
                        generateQuizButton.isVisible = card.stats != null && card.quiz == null

                        finishButton.isEnabled = !isBusy && state.isReadyToFinish
                        discardButton.isEnabled = !isBusy && state.isReadyToFinish
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun populateQuizContainer(container: LinearLayout, quiz: List<QuizQuestion>) {
        if (container.tag == quiz) return
        container.removeAllViews()
        container.tag = quiz

        quiz.forEachIndexed { index, quizQuestion ->
            val questionTextView = TextView(this).apply {
                text = "${index + 1}. ${quizQuestion.question}"
                setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
                setPadding(0, if (index > 0) 24 else 0, 0, 8)
            }
            container.addView(questionTextView)
            val optionsGroup = RadioGroup(this)
            // BOURDON'S FIX: Protect against null options list in a quiz question.
            quizQuestion.options?.forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
                val radioButton = RadioButton(this).apply {
                    text = option
                    if (optionIndex == quizQuestion.correctAnswerIndex) {
                        setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
                        setTextColor(ContextCompat.getColor(this@ForgeActivity, R.color.kikko_success_green))
                    }
                }
                optionsGroup.addView(radioButton)
            }
            container.addView(optionsGroup)
        }
    }

    private fun setupModelSpinner() {
        modelSpinner.onItemSelectedListener = object : AdapterView.OnItemSelectedListener {
            override fun onItemSelected(parent: AdapterView<*>?, view: View?, position: Int, id: Long) {
                (parent?.getItemAtPosition(position) as? String)?.let {
                    if (it != viewModel.uiState.value.selectedModel?.name) {
                        viewModel.onModelSelected(it)
                    }
                }
            }
            override fun onNothingSelected(parent: AdapterView<*>?) {}
        }
    }

    private fun handleImageUris(uris: List<Uri>) {
        lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val bitmaps = uris.mapNotNull { uri ->
                try {
                    contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { BitmapFactory.decodeStream(it) }
                } catch (e: IOException) { null }
            }
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { viewModel.addPollenImages(bitmaps) }
        }
    }

    private fun createTempPhotoUri(): Uri {
        val tempFile = File.createTempFile("pollen_capture_${System.currentTimeMillis()}", ".jpg", cacheDir)
        return FileProvider.getUriForFile(this, "${packageName}.provider", tempFile)
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeActivity.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeLlmHelper.kt ---

// app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeLlmHelper.kt

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.util.Log
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Accelerator
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.MAX_IMAGE_COUNT
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.data.Model
import com.google.mediapipe.framework.image.BitmapImageBuilder
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.GraphOptions
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.LlmInference
import com.google.mediapipe.tasks.genai.llminference.LlmInferenceSession

private const val TAG = "ForgeLlmHelper"

class ForgeLlmHelper(
    // BOURDON'S DEFINITIVE FIX: La visibilité passe de 'private' à 'val' (public par défaut)
    val context: Context,
) {
    private var llmInference: LlmInference? = null
    private var session: LlmInferenceSession? = null

    /**
     * Initialise le moteur et la session avec une configuration de modalité explicite.
     * @param model Le modèle à charger.
     * @param accelerator Le type d'accélérateur (CPU/GPU).
     * @param isMultimodal Vrai si la tâche nécessite le traitement d'images.
     * @return Un message d'erreur ou null en cas de succès.
     */
    fun initialize(model: Model, accelerator: String, isMultimodal: Boolean): String? {
        cleanUp()

        return try {
            val optionsBuilder = LlmInference.LlmInferenceOptions.builder()
                .setModelPath(model.url)
                .setMaxTokens(4096)
                .setPreferredBackend(
                    if (accelerator == Accelerator.GPU.label) LlmInference.Backend.GPU
                    else LlmInference.Backend.CPU
                )
                .setMaxNumImages(if (isMultimodal && model.llmSupportImage) MAX_IMAGE_COUNT else 0)

            val options = optionsBuilder.build()
            llmInference = LlmInference.createFromOptions(context, options)
            session = createNewSession(model, llmInference!!, isMultimodal)
            if (session == null) {
                throw IllegalStateException("Failed to create LlmInferenceSession.")
            }
            null
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            cleanUpMediapipeTaskErrorMessage(e.message ?: "Unknown error during initialization")
        }
    }

    private fun createNewSession(model: Model, llmInference: LlmInference, isMultimodal: Boolean): LlmInferenceSession? {
        return try {
            val sessionOptionsBuilder = LlmInferenceSession.LlmInferenceSessionOptions.builder()
                .setTemperature(0.2f)
                .setTopK(40)

            if (model.llmSupportImage) {
                sessionOptionsBuilder.setGraphOptions(
                    GraphOptions.builder()
                        .setEnableVisionModality(isMultimodal)
                        .build()
                )
            }
            LlmInferenceSession.createFromOptions(llmInference, sessionOptionsBuilder.build())
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to create new session", e)
            null
        }
    }

    fun resetSession(model: Model, isMultimodal: Boolean) {
        try {
            Log.d(TAG, "Resetting session for model '${model.name}' in Forge")
            session?.close()

            val inferenceEngine = llmInference ?: run {
                Log.e(TAG, "Cannot reset session, LlmInference engine is null.")
                return
            }

            session = createNewSession(model, inferenceEngine, isMultimodal)
            Log.d(TAG, "Forge session has been reset successfully.")
        } catch(e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to reset forge session for '${model.name}': ${e.message}")
            cleanUp()
        }
    }

    fun runInference(
        prompt: String,
        images: List<Bitmap>,
        resultListener: (partialResult: String, done: Boolean) -> Unit
    ) {
        val currentSession = session ?: run {
            resultListener("Erreur: Session non initialisée.", true)
            return
        }

        Log.d(TAG, "--- PROMPT ENVOYÉ À LA REINE ---\n$prompt")

        try {
            if (prompt.trim().isNotEmpty()) {
                currentSession.addQueryChunk(prompt)
            }

            if (images.isNotEmpty()) {
                for (image in images) {
                    val mpImage = BitmapImageBuilder(image).build()
                    currentSession.addImage(mpImage)
                }
            }

            val responseBuilder = StringBuilder()
            var isFirstToken = true
            var charsSinceLastLog = 0

            currentSession.generateResponseAsync { partialResult, done ->
                if (isFirstToken) {
                    Log.d(TAG, "--- DÉBUT DU STREAMING DE LA RÉPONSE ---")
                    isFirstToken = false
                }

                responseBuilder.append(partialResult)
                charsSinceLastLog += partialResult.length

                if (charsSinceLastLog >= 50 && !done) {
                    Log.d(TAG, "Streaming... (total: ${responseBuilder.length} caractères)\n--- CONTENU ACTUEL ---\n${responseBuilder.toString()}\n--- FIN CONTENU ---")
                    charsSinceLastLog = 0
                }

                if (done) {
                    Log.i(TAG, "--- RÉPONSE COMPLÈTE REÇUE (${responseBuilder.length} caractères) ---\n${responseBuilder.toString()}")
                }

                resultListener(partialResult, done)
            }

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Erreur durant l'inférence dans la Forge", e)
            resultListener(cleanUpMediapipeTaskErrorMessage(e.message ?: "Error during inference"), true)
        }
    }

    fun cleanUp() {
        session?.close()
        llmInference?.close()
        session = null
        llmInference = null
    }
}

private fun cleanUpMediapipeTaskErrorMessage(message: String): String {
    val index = message.indexOf("=== Source Location Trace")
    if (index >= 0) {
        return message.substring(0, index).trim()
    }
    return message.trim()
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeLlmHelper.kt ---


--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeMlKitHelper.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.util.Log
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.ImageAnalysisReport
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.OcrResult
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.SimpleDetectedObject
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.SimpleImageLabel
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.SwarmAnalysisResult
import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
import com.google.mlkit.common.model.LocalModel
import com.google.mlkit.vision.common.InputImage
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.ImageLabel
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.ImageLabeling
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.custom.CustomImageLabelerOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.label.defaults.ImageLabelerOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.DetectedObject
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.ObjectDetection
import com.google.mlkit.vision.objects.defaults.ObjectDetectorOptions
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.Text
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognition
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.japanese.JapaneseTextRecognizerOptions
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.Closeable

object ForgeMlKitHelper {
    private const val TAG = "ForgeMlKitHelper"

    const val OCR = "ocr"
    const val OBJECT_DETECTION = "object_detection"
    const val DEFAULT_LABELING = "default_labeling"
    const val BIRDS_CLASSIFIER = "birds_classifier"
    const val INSECTS_CLASSIFIER = "insects_classifier"
    const val PLANTS_CLASSIFIER = "plants_classifier"
    const val FOOD_CLASSIFIER = "food_classifier"
    const val MOBILENET_V1 = "mobilenet_v1"
    const val EFFICIENTNET_LITE0 = "efficientnet_lite0"
    const val EFFICIENTNET_LITE1 = "efficientnet_lite1"
    const val EFFICIENTNET_LITE2 = "efficientnet_lite2"
    const val DEEP_OBJECT_ANALYSIS = "deep_object_analysis"

    fun runAnalysis(
        context: Context,
        images: List<Bitmap>,
        enabledModels: Map<String, Boolean>,
        onResult: (SwarmAnalysisResult) -> Unit
    ) {
        Log.d(TAG, "Lancement de l'analyse de l'essaim pour ${images.size} images. Modèles activés: ${enabledModels.filter { it.value }.keys}")
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            val imageReports = mutableListOf<ImageAnalysisReport>()

            images.forEachIndexed { index, bitmap ->
                Log.d(TAG, "Analyse de l'image ${index + 1}/${images.size} de l'essaim...")
                val report = processSingleImage(context, bitmap, enabledModels)
                imageReports.add(report)
            }

            val swarmResult = SwarmAnalysisResult(reports = imageReports)
            Log.i(TAG, "Analyse complète de l'essaim terminée.")

            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                onResult(swarmResult)
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun processSingleImage(context: Context, bitmap: Bitmap, enabledModels: Map<String, Boolean>): ImageAnalysisReport {
        Log.d(TAG, "Traitement d'une seule image...")
        val clientsToClose = mutableListOf<Closeable>()
        var detectedObjects = emptyList<SimpleDetectedObject>()
        val classifierResults = mutableMapOf<String, List<SimpleImageLabel>>()
        var ocrText = ""

        try {
            val image = InputImage.fromBitmap(bitmap, 0)
            val tasks = mutableListOf<com.google.android.gms.tasks.Task<*>>()
            val taskNames = mutableListOf<String>()

            if (enabledModels[OCR] == true) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Ajout de la tâche OCR à l'essaim.")
                val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(JapaneseTextRecognizerOptions.Builder().build())
                clientsToClose.add(recognizer)
                tasks.add(recognizer.process(image))
                taskNames.add(OCR)
            }
            if (enabledModels[OBJECT_DETECTION] == true) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Ajout de la tâche de Détection d'Objets à l'essaim.")
                val options = ObjectDetectorOptions.Builder().setDetectorMode(ObjectDetectorOptions.SINGLE_IMAGE_MODE).enableMultipleObjects().enableClassification().build()
                val detector = ObjectDetection.getClient(options)
                clientsToClose.add(detector)
                tasks.add(detector.process(image))
                taskNames.add(OBJECT_DETECTION)
            }
            if (enabledModels[DEFAULT_LABELING] == true) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Ajout de la tâche de Labellisation par Défaut à l'essaim.")
                val labeler = ImageLabeling.getClient(ImageLabelerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS)
                clientsToClose.add(labeler)
                tasks.add(labeler.process(image))
                taskNames.add("Labels (Défaut)")
            }

            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, BIRDS_CLASSIFIER, "aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-birds-v1-v3.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)
            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, INSECTS_CLASSIFIER, "aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-insects-v1-v3.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)
            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, PLANTS_CLASSIFIER, "aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-plants-v1-v3.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)
            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, FOOD_CLASSIFIER, "aiy-tflite-vision-classifier-food-v1-v1.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)
            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, MOBILENET_V1, "mobilenet_v1_1.0_224_quantized_1_metadata_1.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)
            addCustomClassifierTask(enabledModels, EFFICIENTNET_LITE0, "efficientnet_lite0.tflite", image, tasks, taskNames, clientsToClose)

            if (tasks.isNotEmpty()) {
                val results = Tasks.await(Tasks.whenAllSuccess<Any>(tasks))
                Log.i(TAG, "${results.size} tâches ML Kit terminées avec succès.")

                results.forEachIndexed { index, result ->
                    val taskName = taskNames[index]
                    when (result) {
                        is Text -> ocrText = result.text
                        is List<*> -> {
                            if (result.all { it is DetectedObject }) {
                                detectedObjects = (result as List<DetectedObject>).map { obj ->
                                    SimpleDetectedObject(labels = obj.labels.map { SimpleImageLabel(it.text, it.confidence) })
                                }
                            } else if (result.all { it is ImageLabel }) {
                                classifierResults[taskName] = (result as List<ImageLabel>).map { SimpleImageLabel(it.text, it.confidence) }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "L'une des tâches ML a échoué en mode complexe", e)
        } finally {
            Log.d(TAG, "Nettoyage de ${clientsToClose.size} clients ML Kit.")
            clientsToClose.forEach { it.close() }
        }

        // BOURDON'S FIX: On encapsule le String OCR dans l'objet OcrResult attendu par le modèle de données.
        return ImageAnalysisReport(detectedObjects, classifierResults, OcrResult(fullText = ocrText))
    }

    private fun addCustomClassifierTask(
        enabledModels: Map<String, Boolean>,
        modelKey: String,
        assetPath: String,
        image: InputImage,
        tasks: MutableList<com.google.android.gms.tasks.Task<*>>,
        taskNames: MutableList<String>,
        clientsToClose: MutableList<Closeable>
    ) {
        if (enabledModels[modelKey] == true) {
            Log.d(TAG, "Ajout de la tâche du classifieur personnalisé '$modelKey' à l'essaim.")
            val localModel = LocalModel.Builder().setAssetFilePath(assetPath).build()
            val options = CustomImageLabelerOptions.Builder(localModel).setConfidenceThreshold(0.1f).build()
            val labeler = ImageLabeling.getClient(options)
            clientsToClose.add(labeler)
            tasks.add(labeler.process(image))
            taskNames.add(modelKey.replace("_classifier", "").replaceFirstChar { it.titlecase() })
        }
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/ForgeMlKitHelper.kt ---

    val selectedAccelerator: String = Accelerator.GPU.label,
    val isModelLoading: Boolean = false,

    val isDescriptionReady: Boolean = false,

    val isQuizReady: Boolean = false,

    val isTranslating: Boolean = false,

@HiltViewModel
class ForgeViewModel @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val cardDao: CardDao
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(ForgeUiState())
    val uiState = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    private var llmHelper: ForgeLlmHelper? = null
    private val identificationParser = StreamingJsonParser()
    private val gson = Gson()

    private val allLanguages = listOf(
        Language("ja", "Japonais", 128_000_000),
        Language("fr", "Français", 280_000_000)
    )

    init {
        loadLocalModels()
    }

    private fun loadLocalModels() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val modelsDir = File(context.filesDir, "imported_models")
            val modelFiles = modelsDir.listFiles { _, name -> name.endsWith(".task") } ?: emptyArray()
            val llmModels = modelFiles.map { file ->
                Model(name = file.name, downloadFileName = file.name, url = file.absolutePath, sizeInBytes = file.length(), imported = true, llmSupportImage = file.name.contains("gemma-3n", ignoreCase = true))
            }.sortedBy { it.name }

            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(availableModels = llmModels, availableLanguagesForTranslation = allLanguages) }
                llmModels.firstOrNull()?.let { onModelSelected(it.name) } ?: _uiState.update { it.copy(statusText = "Aucun modèle local trouvé.") }
            }
        }
    }


    fun onModelSelected(modelName: String) {
        val model = _uiState.value.availableModels.find { it.name == modelName }
        if (model == null || (model == _uiState.value.selectedModel && llmHelper != null)) return

        _uiState.update { it.copy(isModelLoading = true, statusText = "Initialisation...", pollenImages = emptyList(), selectedModel = model) }
        resetFullState()

        llmHelper?.cleanUp()
        llmHelper = ForgeLlmHelper(context)
/*
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val error = llmHelper?.initialize(model, _uiState.value.selectedAccelerator)
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isModelLoading = false, statusText = if (error == null) "'${model.name}' est prête." else "Erreur: $error", selectedModel = if (error == null) model else null) }
            }
        }*/
    }

    fun onAcceleratorSelected(accelerator: String) {
        if (accelerator == _uiState.value.selectedAccelerator) return
        _uiState.update { it.copy(selectedAccelerator = accelerator) }
        _uiState.value.selectedModel?.let { onModelSelected(it.name) }
    }

    fun addPollenImages(images: List<Bitmap>) {
        val currentPollen = _uiState.value.pollenImages.toMutableList()
        currentPollen.addAll(images)
        val finalPollen = if (currentPollen.size > MAX_IMAGE_COUNT) currentPollen.take(MAX_IMAGE_COUNT) else currentPollen
        resetFullState()
        _uiState.update { it.copy(pollenImages = finalPollen) }
    }

    fun removePollenImage(index: Int) {
        val currentPollen = _uiState.value.pollenImages.toMutableList()
        if (index in currentPollen.indices) {
            currentPollen.removeAt(index)
            resetFullState()
            _uiState.update { it.copy(pollenImages = currentPollen) }
        }
    }

    fun runMlKitAnalysis(enabledModels: Map<String, Boolean>) {
        if (_uiState.value.pollenImages.isEmpty()) {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(statusText = "Veuillez d'abord récolter du pollen.") }
            return
        }
        resetFullState(keepPollen = true)
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isAnalyzingMlKit = true, statusText = "Les Abeilles analysent...") }

        ForgeMlKitHelper.runAnalysis(context, _uiState.value.pollenImages, enabledModels) { swarmResult ->
            val readableReport = GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create().toJson(swarmResult)
            // BOURDON'S FIX: Appel de la nouvelle méthode renommée dans le PromptGenerator
         //   val finalPrompt = PromptGenerator.generateMultimodalSwarmSynthesisPrompt(swarmResult)
          //  _uiState.update { it.copy(isAnalyzingMlKit = false, statusText = "Rapport terminé. Prêt pour l'identification.", mlKitReport = readableReport, generatedPrompt = finalPrompt, swarmAnalysisResult = swarmResult) }
        }
    }

    fun runIdentification(prompt: String) {
        if (_uiState.value.isInferring || _uiState.value.pollenImages.isEmpty()) return
        identificationParser.reset()
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isInferring = true, statusText = "Étape 1: La Reine identifie...", streamingCharCount = 0) }
        Log.d(TAG, "--- PROMPT POUR IDENTIFICATION ---\n$prompt")

        val responseBuilder = StringBuilder()
        llmHelper?.runInference(prompt, _uiState.value.pollenImages) { partialResult, done ->
            responseBuilder.append(partialResult)
            _uiState.update { it.copy(streamingCharCount = responseBuilder.length) }
            if (done) {
                Log.i(TAG, "--- RÉPONSE BRUTE DE L'IDENTIFICATION ---\n${responseBuilder.toString()}")
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    val finalResult = identificationParser.process(responseBuilder.toString())
                    if (finalResult.isComplete) {
                        val imageToSave = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { _uiState.value.pollenImages.firstOrNull()?.let { saveBitmapToPermanentStorage(it) } }
                        val newCard = KnowledgeCard(
                            specificName = finalResult.specificName,
                            deckName = finalResult.deckName,
                            imagePath = imageToSave,
                            confidence = finalResult.confidence,
                            reasoning = Reasoning(visualAnalysis = finalResult.reasoning["visualAnalysis"] ?: "", evidenceCorrelation = finalResult.reasoning["evidenceCorrelation"] ?: ""),
                            description = null, stats = null, allergens = null, ingredients = null, quiz = null, translations = null
                        )
                        val newId = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { cardDao.insert(newCard) }
                        val savedCard = newCard.copy(id = newId)
                        _uiState.update { it.copy(isInferring = false, statusText = "Carte créée ! Prêt pour l'étape 2.", streamingCharCount = 0, currentCardId = newId, forgedCard = savedCard, isIdentificationReady = true, isReadyToFinish = true) }
                    } else {
                        _uiState.update { it.copy(isInferring = false, streamingCharCount = 0, statusText = "Erreur: La réponse de la Reine était incomplète.") }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun generateDescription() {
        val cardId = _uiState.value.currentCardId ?: return
        if (_uiState.value.isInferring) return
/*

    fun discardForgedResult() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            _uiState.value.currentCardId?.let { cardId ->
                cardDao.getCardById(cardId)?.let { cardDao.delete(it) }
            }
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                resetFullState()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun resetFullState(keepPollen: Boolean = false) {
        _uiState.update { currentState ->
            /*
            currentState.selectedModel?.let {
                llmHelper?.resetSession(it)
            }

             */
            ForgeUiState(
                pollenImages = if (keepPollen) currentState.pollenImages else emptyList(),
                availableModels = currentState.availableModels,
                selectedModel = currentState.selectedModel,
                selectedAccelerator = currentState.selectedAccelerator,
                statusText = if (currentState.selectedModel != null) "'${currentState.selectedModel?.name}' est prête." else "Veuillez sélectionner un modèle."
            )
        }
    }

    private suspend fun saveBitmapToPermanentStorage(bitmap: Bitmap): String? {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val cardImagesDir = File(context.filesDir, "card_images")
                if (!cardImagesDir.exists()) cardImagesDir.mkdirs()
                val permanentFile = File(cardImagesDir, "card_${UUID.randomUUID()}.png")
                FileOutputStream(permanentFile).use { out ->
                    bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, out)
                }
                permanentFile.absolutePath
            } catch (e: IOException) {
                e.printStackTrace()
                null
            }
        }
    }

    private fun parseStats(jsonString: String): CardStats? {
        return try {
            val firstBrace = jsonString.indexOf('{')
            val lastBrace = jsonString.lastIndexOf('}')
            if (firstBrace == -1 || lastBrace == -1) return null
            val cleanJson = jsonString.substring(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)
            val type = object : TypeToken<Map<String, String>>() {}.type
            CardStats(title = "Statistics", items = gson.fromJson(cleanJson, type))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Erreur de parsing des stats simples: $jsonString", e)
            null
        }
    }

    private fun parseFoodStatsResult(jsonString: String): FoodStatsResult? {
        return try {
            val firstBrace = jsonString.indexOf('{')
            val lastBrace = jsonString.lastIndexOf('}')
            if (firstBrace == -1 || lastBrace == -1) return null
            val cleanJson = jsonString.substring(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)
            gson.fromJson(cleanJson, FoodStatsResult::class.java)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Erreur de parsing des stats FOOD: $jsonString", e)
            null
        }
    }

    private fun parseQuiz(jsonString: String): List<QuizQuestion> {
        return try {
            val firstBracket = jsonString.indexOf('[')
            val lastBracket = jsonString.lastIndexOf(']')
            if (firstBracket == -1 || lastBracket == -1) return emptyList()
            val cleanJson = jsonString.substring(firstBracket, lastBracket + 1)
            val type = object : TypeToken<List<QuizQuestion>>() {}.type
            gson.fromJson(cleanJson, type) ?: emptyList()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Erreur de parsing du quiz: $jsonString", e)
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        llmHelper?.cleanUp()
        TtsService.shutdown()
    }
}

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageButton
import android.widget.ImageView
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R

/**
 * Un adaptateur pour le RecyclerView qui affiche une prévisualisation horizontale
 * des images (pollen) sélectionnées par l'utilisateur.
 *
 * @param pollenImages La liste mutable des bitmaps à afficher.
 * @param onRemoveClick Une fonction lambda appelée lorsqu'un utilisateur clique sur le bouton de suppression
 * d'une image, passant l'index de l'image à supprimer.
 */
class PollenPreviewAdapter(
    private val pollenImages: MutableList<Bitmap>,
    private val onRemoveClick: (Int) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<PollenPreviewAdapter.PollenViewHolder>() {

    /**
     * ViewHolder qui contient les vues pour un seul item de prévisualisation de pollen.
     */
    inner class PollenViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
        val imageView: ImageView = view.findViewById(R.id.pollen_preview_imageview)
       /* val removeButton//: ImageButton = view.findViewById(R.id.pollen_remove_button)

        init {
            removeButton.setOnClickListener {
                // S'assure que la position est valide avant de déclencher le callback
                val position = adapterPosition
                if (position != RecyclerView.NO_POSITION) {
                    onRemoveClick(position)
                }
            }
        }

        */
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): PollenViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            .inflate(R.layout.item_pollen_preview, parent, false)
        return PollenViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: PollenViewHolder, position: Int) {
        val bitmap = pollenImages[position]
        holder.imageView.setImageBitmap(bitmap)
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = pollenImages.size

    /**
     * Met à jour la liste des images affichées par l'adaptateur.
     * @param newImages La nouvelle liste de bitmaps.
     */
    fun updateImages(newImages: List<Bitmap>) {
        pollenImages.clear()
        pollenImages.addAll(newImages)
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/PromptGenerator.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.CardStats
import com.google.gson.Gson
import java.util.Locale

object PromptGenerator {

    private val deckStats = mapOf(
        "Bird" to listOf("Wingspan (cm)", "Average Weight (g)", "Lifespan (years)", "Clutch Size (eggs)"),
        "Insect" to listOf("Average Length (mm)", "Lifespan (days)", "Number of Legs", "Flying Speed (km/h)"),
        "Plant" to listOf("Maximum Height (m)", "Flowering Period (months)", "Lifespan (years)", "Minimum Temperature (°C)"),
        "Food" to listOf("Energy (kcal per 100g)", "Protein (g per 100g)")
    )

    private val majorAllergensList = listOf(
        "Cereals containing gluten", "Crustaceans", "Eggs", "Fish", "Peanuts",
        "Soybeans", "Milk", "Nuts", "Celery", "Mustard", "Sesame seeds",
        "Sulphur dioxide and sulphites", "Lupin", "Molluscs"
    )

    // PROMPT ÉTAPE 1 : Identification
    fun generateIdentificationPrompt(reportsJson: String): String {
        val validDecks = "`Bird`, `Insect`, `Plant`, `Food`"

        return """
        ROLE: You are the AI Queen of the Kikko Hive, an expert in multimodal synthesis. Your reasoning must be flawless and strictly follow the evidence provided.

        CONTEXT: You are receiving a JSON **array** of intelligence reports. Each object in the array corresponds to one visual clue (image). The `ocr_results` are the most reliable source of truth when available.

        TASK: **Synthesize ALL reports in the array** to determine a **single, unified subject identity**.
        1.  **Evidence Prioritization (CRITICAL):**
            -   **IF** the `ocr_results.full_text` from any report contains a specific product name or species: This OCR data is your **PRIMARY EVIDENCE**.
            -   **ELSE**: Use the highest-confidence labels from other classifiers across all reports as your PRIMARY EVIDENCE.
        2.  **Final Ruling:** Synthesize all information to determine one final `specificName` and one final `deckName`.

        CRITICAL RULES:
        -   You MUST IGNORE low-confidence labels that contradict the primary evidence.
        -   If the evidence is inconclusive, respond with `{"error": "Inconclusive analysis"}`.

        OUTPUT FORMAT: Your response MUST be a **single, valid JSON object**. Do NOT add any other text or markdown.
        {
          "reasoning": {
            "visualAnalysis": "Your brief analysis synthesizing what is visible across ALL images.",
            "evidenceCorrelation": "Your analysis of the JSON reports, explaining how you combined evidence from all reports to reach a single conclusion."
          },
         "specificName": "The most specific and functional name of the subject.",
         "deckName": "based on the specificName, select the single most appropriate deck name from this short list: [$validDecks] ",
          "confidence": 0.95
        }

        --- START OF DATA ---
        [INTELLIGENCE REPORTS]:
        $reportsJson
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    // PROMPT ÉTAPE 2 : Description
    fun generateDescriptionPrompt(subject: String, deckName: String): String {
        val lowerSubject = subject.lowercase(Locale.getDefault())
        return """
        ROLE: You are a creative storyteller. Based on the subject provided, your task is to continue our session.
        TASK: Write a SHORT descriptive paragraph (3 to 5 sentences) about "$deckName - $lowerSubject". Include its origins and two interesting facts.
        The response must be a single paragraph of plain text, in English.
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    // PROMPT ÉTAPE 3 : Stats
    fun generateStatsExtractionPrompt(specificName: String, deckName: String, ocrText: String, description: String): String {
        val statsToFind = deckStats[deckName]
        if (statsToFind == null && deckName != "Food") return ""

        val statsString = statsToFind?.joinToString(", ") ?: ""
        val lowerSpecificName = specificName.lowercase(Locale.getDefault())
        val isFood = deckName == "Food"
        val topLevelKeys = if (isFood) "\"stats\", \"allergens\", \"ingredients\"" else "\"stats\""

        return """
        ROLE: You are a meticulous Data Extraction AI. To continue our session, analyze the provided context.

        CONTEXT: You are analyzing "$lowerSpecificName". You have the `[RAW_OCR_DATA]` from the initial analysis and the `[NARRATIVE_DESCRIPTION]` we just generated.

        TASK: Find values for: $statsString. ${if (isFood) "Also find `allergens` from `[ALLERGEN_LIST]` and the `ingredients` list." else ""}

        SEARCH PRIORITY:
        1.  **OCR**: Prioritize precise figures from `[RAW_OCR_DATA]`.
        2.  **NARRATIVE**: Search `[NARRATIVE_DESCRIPTION]` if not in OCR.
        3.  **INFERENCE**: If still not found, you MUST provide a typical average value for "$lowerSpecificName".

        OUTPUT FORMAT: A single, valid JSON object with top-level keys: $topLevelKeys. For "allergens" and "ingredients", return an empty list `[]` if none are found. Do not add markdown or other text.
        **IMPORTANT**: Never use double quotes `"` inside a JSON string value. Use single quotes `'` instead.

        --- START OF DATA ---
        [ALLERGEN_LIST]: [${majorAllergensList.joinToString(", ")}]
        [RAW_OCR_DATA]: "$ocrText"
        [NARRATIVE_DESCRIPTION]: "$description"
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    // PROMPT ÉTAPE 4 : Quiz (Nouvelle Session)
    fun generateQuizPrompt(subject: String, description: String, stats: CardStats?): String {
        val statsJson = if (stats != null) Gson().toJson(stats.items) else "{}"
        return """
        ROLE: You are a creative but strictly factual Quiz Master AI.

        CONTEXT: You have a `[DESCRIPTION]` and `[STATS_DATA]` about a subject. This is your ONLY source of truth.

        TASK: Create exactly 4 multiple-choice questions with 4 answers each from a mix of BOTH sources. At least one question must be about a specific numerical value from the stats.

        CRITICAL RULE: DO NOT use any external knowledge. All questions and answers must be directly verifiable from the provided context.

        OUTPUT FORMAT: A single, valid JSON array. Do not add any other explanatory text.
        Format: `[
        {"q": "...", 
        "o": ["...", "...", "...", "..."], 
        "c": correct_index, 
        "explanation": "..."},
        {"q": "...", 
        "o": ["...", "...", "...", "..."], 
        "c": correct_index, 
        "explanation": "..."},
        {"q": "...", 
        "o": ["...", "...", "...", "..."], 
        "c": correct_index, 
        "explanation": "..."},
        {"q": "...", 
        "o": ["...", "...", "...", "..."], 
        "c": correct_index, 
        "explanation": "..."}
        ]`
        **IMPORTANT**: Never use double quotes `"` inside a JSON string value. Use single quotes `'` or other characters instead.

        --- START OF DATA ---
        [DESCRIPTION]: "$description"
        [STATS_DATA]: $statsJson
        """.trimIndent()
    }
}

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import androidx.appcompat.widget.Toolbar
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.R
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.PollenStatus
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.persistence.PollenGrainDao
import com.google.android.material.card.MaterialCardView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * Activité servant de tableau de bord pour visualiser l'état de la Forge.
 * Affiche le nombre de grains de pollen pour chaque statut (RAW, FORGING, FORGED, ERROR).
 */
class StartForgeActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var pollenGrainDao: PollenGrainDao

    // Vues pour les compteurs
    private lateinit var rawCounterTextView: TextView
    private lateinit var forgingCounterTextView: TextView
    private lateinit var forgedCounterTextView: TextView
    private lateinit var errorCounterTextView: TextView

    // Vues pour les cartes cliquables
    private lateinit var rawCard: MaterialCardView
    private lateinit var forgingCard: MaterialCardView
    private lateinit var forgedCard: MaterialCardView
    private lateinit var errorCard: MaterialCardView

    private lateinit var toolbar: Toolbar

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_start_forge)

        pollenGrainDao = PollenGrainDao(this)

        bindViews()
        setupToolbar()
        setupListeners()
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        // Rafraîchit les compteurs chaque fois que l'écran devient visible.
        updateCounters()
    }

    private fun bindViews() {
        toolbar = findViewById(R.id.toolbar)
        rawCounterTextView = findViewById(R.id.raw_counter_textview)
        forgingCounterTextView = findViewById(R.id.forging_counter_textview)
        forgedCounterTextView = findViewById(R.id.forged_counter_textview)
        errorCounterTextView = findViewById(R.id.error_counter_textview)

        rawCard = findViewById(R.id.card_status_raw)
        forgingCard = findViewById(R.id.card_status_forging)
        forgedCard = findViewById(R.id.card_status_forged)
        errorCard = findViewById(R.id.card_status_error)
    }

    private fun setupToolbar() {
        toolbar.setNavigationOnClickListener {
            finish() // Ferme l'activité
        }
    }

    private fun setupListeners() {
        rawCard.setOnClickListener {
            // TODO: Implémenter la navigation vers une liste de grains RAW
            Toast.makeText(this, "Affichage des grains de Pollen Brut", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
        forgingCard.setOnClickListener {
            // TODO: Implémenter la navigation vers une liste de grains FORGING
            Toast.makeText(this, "Affichage des grains en cours de Forge", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
        forgedCard.setOnClickListener {
            // TODO: Implémenter la navigation vers la liste des cartes (Miel)
            Toast.makeText(this, "Affichage du Miel déjà Forgé", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
        errorCard.setOnClickListener {
            // TODO: Implémenter la navigation vers une liste de grains en ERREUR
            Toast.makeText(this, "Affichage des Forges en erreur", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    private fun updateCounters() {
        lifecycleScope.launch {
            // Récupère les comptes depuis la DB sur un thread d'arrière-plan
            val counts = pollenGrainDao.countByStatus()

            // Met à jour l'UI sur le thread principal
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                // BOURDON'S FIX: Le compteur "En Forge" est la somme de tous les états intermédiaires.
                val forgingCount = (counts[PollenStatus.IDENTIFYING] ?: 0) +
                        (counts[PollenStatus.PENDING_DESCRIPTION] ?: 0) +
                        (counts[PollenStatus.PENDING_STATS] ?: 0) +
                        (counts[PollenStatus.PENDING_QUIZ] ?: 0) +
                        (counts[PollenStatus.PENDING_TRANSLATION] ?: 0)

                rawCounterTextView.text = (counts[PollenStatus.RAW] ?: 0).toString()
                forgingCounterTextView.text = forgingCount.toString()
                forgedCounterTextView.text = (counts[PollenStatus.FORGED] ?: 0).toString()
                errorCounterTextView.text = (counts[PollenStatus.ERROR] ?: 0).toString()
            }
        }
    }
}

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/StreamingJsonParser.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.JsonSyntaxException
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model.IdentificationResult

/**
 * Un analyseur JSON intelligent et tolérant aux pannes, conçu pour interpréter
 * la sortie parfois imparfaite des LLMs en streaming.
 */
class StreamingJsonParser {

    private var buffer = ""
    private val gson = Gson()

    /**
     * Contient le résultat partiel ou complet d'une analyse JSON en streaming.
     */
    data class ParsedResult(
        val specificName: String = "",
        val deckName: String = "",
        val reasoning: Map<String, String> = emptyMap(),
        val confidence: Float = 0.0f,
        val isComplete: Boolean = false
    )

    fun process(chunk: String): ParsedResult {
        buffer += chunk

        // Tentative 1: Essayer de parser le JSON le plus complet possible.
        val firstBrace = buffer.indexOf('{')
        val lastBrace = buffer.lastIndexOf('}')

        if (firstBrace != -1 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
            val potentialJson = buffer.substring(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)
            try {
                // Essai avec Gson, le plus strict
                val result: IdentificationResult = gson.fromJson(potentialJson, IdentificationResult::class.java)
                if (result.specificName.isNotBlank() && result.deckName.isNotBlank()) {
                    return ParsedResult(
                        specificName = result.specificName,
                        deckName = result.deckName,
                        reasoning = mapOf(
                            "visualAnalysis" to result.reasoning.visualAnalysis,
                            "evidenceCorrelation" to result.reasoning.evidenceCorrelation
                        ),
                        confidence = result.confidence,
                        isComplete = true
                    )
                }
            } catch (e: JsonSyntaxException) {
                // Le JSON est malformé. On passe à la tentative 2.
            }
        }

        // Tentative 2: Extraction manuelle des champs clés, tolérante aux erreurs.
        val specificName = extractStringValue("specificName")
        val deckName = extractStringValue("deckName")

        val isPotentiallyComplete = specificName.isNotBlank() && deckName.isNotBlank() && buffer.contains("confidence")

        return ParsedResult(
            specificName = specificName,
            deckName = deckName,
            isComplete = isPotentiallyComplete
        )
    }

    private fun extractStringValue(key: String): String {
        val keyPattern = "\"$key\"\\s*:\\s*\"(.*?)\""
        val regex = keyPattern.toRegex()
        val match = regex.find(buffer)
        return match?.groups?.get(1)?.value ?: ""
    }

    fun reset() {
        buffer = ""
    }
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/forge/StreamingJsonParser.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.forge

/**
 * Contient le résultat complet d'une analyse d'essaim sur plusieurs images.
 * @param reports La liste des rapports d'analyse pour chaque image.
 */
data class SwarmAnalysisResult(
    val reports: List<ImageAnalysisReport>
)

/**
 * Contient le rapport d'analyse d'une seule image par les Abeilles Spécialistes.
 * @param detectedObjects La liste des objets détectés.
 * @param globalClassifierResults Une map des résultats des classifieurs, où la clé est le nom du classifieur.
 * @param ocrText Le texte brut reconnu par l'OCR.
 */
data class ImageAnalysisReport(
    val detectedObjects: List<SimpleDetectedObject>,
    val globalClassifierResults: Map<String, List<SimpleImageLabel>>,
    val ocrText: String
)

/**
 * Une représentation simplifiée d'un objet détecté par ML Kit.
 * @param labels La liste des labels associés à cet objet.
 */
data class SimpleDetectedObject(
    val labels: List<SimpleImageLabel>
)

/**
 * Une représentation simplifiée d'un label d'image (pour les objets ou les classifieurs).
 * @param text Le nom du label (ex: "Cat", "Eiffel Tower").
 * @param confidence Le score de confiance de la détection (entre 0.0 et 1.0).
 */
data class SimpleImageLabel(
    val text: String,
    val confidence: Float
)

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model

import android.os.Parcelable
import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName
import kotlinx.parcelize.Parcelize

/**
 * Contient tous les champs traduisibles pour une langue spécifique.
 */
@Serializable
@Parcelize
data class TranslatedContent(
    val description: String?,
    val reasoning: Reasoning?,
    val quiz: List<QuizQuestion>?
) : Parcelable


/**
 * Représente une seule pièce de connaissance.
 * BOURDON'S REFACTOR: Les champs générés après l'identification sont maintenant nullable
 * pour permettre une sauvegarde progressive.
 */
@Serializable
@Parcelize
data class KnowledgeCard(
    val id: Long = 0,
    val specificName: String,
    val deckName: String,
    val imagePath: String?,
    val confidence: Float,
    // Contenu original (EN)
    val reasoning: Reasoning,
    val description: String?,
    val stats: CardStats?,
    val allergens: List<String>?,
    val ingredients: List<String>?,
    val quiz: List<QuizQuestion>?,
    val translations: Map<String, TranslatedContent>?
) : Parcelable

@Serializable
@Parcelize
data class Reasoning(
    val visualAnalysis: String,
    val evidenceCorrelation: String
) : Parcelable

@Serializable
@Parcelize
data class CardStats(
    val title: String,
    val items: Map<String, String>
) : Parcelable

@Serializable
@Parcelize
data class QuizQuestion(
    // BOURDON'S FIX: Mapping the short JSON keys to the data class properties.
    @SerializedName("q")
    val question: String,
    @SerializedName("o")
    val options: List<String>,
    @SerializedName("c")
    val correctAnswerIndex: Int,
    // BOURDON'S FIX: Made explanation nullable as it's not always provided by the AI.
    val explanation: String?
) : Parcelable

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model

import android.os.Parcelable
import kotlinx.parcelize.Parcelize
import java.util.UUID

/**
 * Représente une session de récolte de "pollen" complète.
 * C'est un POKO (Plain Old Kotlin Object) utilisé comme modèle de données
 * pour la persistance en SQL pur.
 *
 * @property id Identifiant unique de la session de récolte (UUID).
 * @property timestamp Horodatage de la fin de la capture.
 * @property status L'état actuel du grain dans le cycle de vie de la Forge.
 * @property userIntent L'intention vocale ou textuelle de l'utilisateur lors de la capture.
 * @property pollenImagePaths Les chemins d'accès vers les fichiers images sources de la capture. Sérialisé en JSON.
 * @property swarmAnalysisReportJson Le rapport JSON brut complet de l'analyse par les Abeilles Spécialistes.
 * @property forgedCardId L'ID de la KnowledgeCard résultante une fois la Forge terminée.
 */
@Parcelize
data class PollenGrain(
    val id: String = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    var status: PollenStatus = PollenStatus.RAW, // 'var' pour pouvoir le mettre à jour
    val userIntent: String?,
    val pollenImagePaths: List<String>,
    val swarmAnalysisReportJson: String?,
    var forgedCardId: Long? = null // 'var' pour pouvoir le mettre à jour
) : Parcelable

--- START OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/model/PollenStatus.kt ---

package be.heyman.android.ai.kikko.model

/**
 * Définit les différents états possibles pour un PollenGrain dans son cycle de vie,
 * de sa capture brute à sa transformation finale en Miel (KnowledgeCard).
 * Chaque état représente une étape dans la chaîne de production de notre WorkManager.
 */
enum class PollenStatus {
    /**
     * Le grain de pollen vient d'être capturé et est prêt pour la Forge.
     */
    RAW,

    /**
     * Étape 1 : Le grain est en cours d'identification par la Reine IA.
     */
    IDENTIFYING,

    /**
     * En attente de l'Étape 2 : La génération de la description narrative.
     */
    PENDING_DESCRIPTION,

    /**
     * En attente de l'Étape 3 : L'extraction des statistiques.
     */
    PENDING_STATS,

    /**
     * En attente de l'Étape 4 : La création du quiz.
     */
    PENDING_QUIZ,

    /**
     * En attente de l'Étape 5 : La traduction du contenu.
     */
    PENDING_TRANSLATION,

    /**
     * Le processus de Forge est terminé avec succès. La KnowledgeCard est complète.
     */
    FORGED,

    /**
     * Une erreur est survenue à l'une des étapes de la Forge.
     */
    ERROR
}

--- END OF FILE app/src/main/java/be/heyman/android/ai/kikko/model/PollenStatus.kt ---

data class SwarmAnalysisResult(
    val reports: List<ImageAnalysisReport>?

 * BOURDON'S ROBUSTNESS FIX V3:
 * - Structure de données alignée sur le JSON réel de PollenForge.
 * - Toutes les collections et propriétés potentiellement manquantes sont nullables.
 */
data class ImageAnalysisReport(
    @SerializedName("object_detection_results")
    val detectedObjects: List<SimpleDetectedObject>?,

    @SerializedName("global_classification_results")
    val globalClassifierResults: Map<String, List<SimpleImageLabel>?>?,

    @SerializedName("ocr_results")
    val ocrResults: OcrResult?
) {
    val ocrText: String
        get() = ocrResults?.fullText ?: ""
}

    @SerializedName("full_text")
    val fullText: String?
)

    val labels: List<SimpleImageLabel>
)

    // BOURDON'S FIX: Rendu nullable pour empêcher les crashs de Gson/Kotlin.
